([thiagoedson/claude-frontforge](https://github.com/thiagoedson/claude-frontforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/audit.md) · ★1)

([thiagoedson/claude-frontforge](https://github.com/thiagoedson/claude-frontforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/colorize.md) · ★1)
- **claude-frontforge:component**: Build a UI component (Button, Card, Modal, Input, Table, Dropdown, etc.) with all variants, interactive states (hover, focus, disabled, loading), accessibility attributes, and design token references — consistent with the project's established design system. - **claude-frontforge:critique**: UX heuristic review of a screen or flow — scores hierarchy, cognitive load, feedback, error prevention, and consistency, and returns a prioritized punch list. ([thiagoedson/claude-frontforge](https://github.com/thiagoedson/claude-frontforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/critique.md) · ★1)
